## Semantic findings

Every declaration-doc record received automated lexical, attachment, reachability,
duplicate, status/trust-word, numeric/cardinality, branch, radius/source-role, and
generated-provenance screening. Manual checking then covered the complete confirmed
and conservative finding set below, all ten kernel-claim docs, all malformed/wrong-
opener candidates, the FrontierLiveClosure and Census554 review lanes, and the large
generated naming templates. An automated flag is a routing signal, not a verdict.

### Disposition totals

| Population | Confirmed mismatch | Conservative review | Validated clean | Automated-only or separate disposition |
|---|---:|---:|---:|---:|
| 21667 source-attached declaration `/--` records | 10 records | 10 records (6 finding groups) | 4061 records | 17586 automated-only records |
| 3 command-adjacent lexical `/--` records | 0 | 0 | 0 | 3 manually validated command docs with no declaration target |
| 3287 module `/-!` records | 1 | 11 optional style reviews | 21 adjacency candidates | 3254 separate module-census records |
| malformed `/-/` blocks (outside JSONL) | 4 | 0 | 0 | 0 |

The 4061 clean declaration records comprise 3947 generated name/range templates, 108
docs whose native-decision trust wording was checked, and six other kernel-claim docs
checked against source/proof closure. “Validated clean” is scoped to the claim tested;
for example, template validation confirms the documented generator/range against the
FQN, not every coefficient value in the declaration body. The 17586 automated-only
records remain explicitly marked as not manually validated in JSONL.

Attachment has a separate verification axis: 4911 declaration docs are outside the
active aggregate import graph and therefore have no aggregate-environment verdict;
ten reachable docs precede anonymous `example` commands, which create no named
environment constant. These 4921 records are not silently called environment-verified.

### Complete risk-filtered finding table

No suspected mismatch is omitted from this table. R001-R006 are conservative semantic
or pre-import risks. M005 and M007-M016 are optional low-priority opener/style reviews:
their prose may intentionally be module narrative and is not classified as a defect.

| ID | Source doc/comment → target | Verdict | Severity | Decisive mismatch | Proposed replacement or correction |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| D001 | `lean/Erdos9796Proof/P97/Census554/BaseSat.lean:117` → `Problem97.Census554.BaseSat.baseAssign` at 118 | confirmed | medium | “exactly the eleven” is not guaranteed for unrestricted `idx`; out-of-range choices and `xVar` collisions are permitted by the definition | “The base assignment sets each listed chosen `x` variable to true.” Add “exactly eleven” only with the later bounds/distinctness hypotheses. |
| D002 | `lean/Erdos9796Proof/P97/Census554/CoverIndexBridge.lean:28` → `Problem97.Census554.CoverIndexBridge.classMask_mem_candMasks` at 30 | confirmed | medium | “exactly when” asserts both directions, while the theorem only proves `IsCandidateClass p K → classMask K ∈ candMasks p.val` | “A semantic candidate class has a mask in the encoder's candidate enumeration for that center.” |
| D003 | `lean/Erdos9796Proof/P97/Census554/CapSelectedNogoodCertificate.lean:307` → `Problem97.Census554.CapSelectedNogoodCertificate.hasNogood` at 308 | confirmed | medium | the definition checks only `matchesRows`; it does not require `NogoodData.check` or `checkBank`, so “certified” is stronger than the declaration | “The supplied bank contains at least one matching nogood record.” State certification separately under a checked-bank premise. |
| D004 | `lean/Erdos9796Proof/P97/Census554/EquilateralShellBranchCertificates.lean:706` → `Problem97.Census554.EquilateralShellBranchCertificates.not_pentagonalAntiprismBranchSystem` at 707 | confirmed | medium | the doc omits the material hypothesis `ha : a ^ 2 = 3` | “Under `a ^ 2 = 3`, none of the 32 exact pentagonal-antiprism branch systems has a real solution.” |
| D005 | `lean/Erdos9796Proof/P97/Census554/CirclePowerMatrix.lean:63` → `Problem97.Census554.CirclePowerMatrix.power_center_ne_zero` at 65 | confirmed | medium | “positive-radius” is not the theorem hypothesis; the theorem assumes only `radiusSq ≠ 0`, which also allows negative parameters | “If `radiusSq ≠ 0`, then `power center radiusSq center ≠ 0`.” |

The four native-replay axiom probes D007-D010 all reported the full closure
`[propext, Classical.choice, Lean.ofReduceBool, Lean.trustCompiler, Quot.sound]`.
The six other kernel-claim docs were retained as clean: the active exact-four and
shared-pair declarations had only the ordinary `propext`/choice/quotient closure; the
two `CheckpointedRup` declarations used kernel `decide`; the U5 doc explicitly speaks
about a future certificate; and the unreachable exact-twelve mapping has a direct
`by decide` body (source-validated, without an aggregate-environment claim).

### Generated-template and native-trust validation

- All 2729 docs of the form “Computed product block for generator …, coefficient terms
  … through …” matched the zero-padded generator/range suffix of their attached FQN.
- All 1218 endpoint “Computed coefficient block” docs matched the named endpoint and
  zero-padded block/range suffix of their attached FQN.
- All 108 declaration docs that explicitly mention `native_decide` or native-decision
  trust were checked as honest about the native compiler boundary. In particular, 106
  checked-identity templates name `Lean.ofReduceBool`/`Lean.trustCompiler`; the two
  structural/certificate summaries do not claim kernel-only closure.
- The full frozen source tree contains 59 literal `sorry` occurrences. Status-language
  screening found no other high-confidence “closed/proved/unconditional” claim attached
  to a declaration whose own body directly contains `sorry`; D006 is the confirmed
  transitive-closure exception.

## Verification limits

This audit changes no Lean source and makes no theorem-promotion claim. A source scan,
even when exhaustive over the frozen manifest, does not replace a fresh build, final-
consumer `#print axioms`, evaluated-runtime trust audit, or publication gate.
